みずほ経済 EXPRESS

みずほ GDP ナウ（2026 年 10 月中旬時点） 
～7～9 月期 GDP は前期比＋0.03％（年率＋0.10％）と推計～

調査部  シニア日本経済エコノミスト 越山祐資 
同             エコノミスト  阿部大樹 
080-1069-4835 yusuke.koshiyama@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 10 月中旬時点の「みずほ GDP ナウ」は 7～9 月期 GDP を前期比＋0.10％と予測 
みずほ総合研究所では景気動向をタイムリーに把握するべく、浦沢（2023）等を参考にGDPナウキ
ャスティングに取り組んできた。これを「みずほGDPナウ」と呼称することとし、太田他（2024）で
はデータドリブン予測である「みずほGDPナウ」が民間予測平均並みの予測精度を確保できることを
示した。その上で、酒井他（2024）をはじめとして、月次経済指標を用いたGDPナウキャスティング
の結果を紹介してきたところである。 
本稿では10月中旬時点までに得られる月次経済指標を用い、 「みずほGDPナウ」 による7～9月期GDP
の予測結果を紹介する。予測に用いたデータは、8月分の鉱工業生産、 消費活動指数、 所定外労働時間、
消費財出荷指数、第3次産業活動指数、9月分の中小企業景況調査（売上げ見通しDI）である1。図表1
には、予測に使用した各月次経済指標の推移を示している。 
図表 1 各月次経済指標の推移

### Page 2

モデルによる10月中旬時点における7 ～9月期実質GDP成長率の予測値は、図表2のとおり前期比＋
0.03％ （年率＋0.10％）となった。 他機関による予測では、東京財団政策研究所による10月15日時点の
ナウキャスティングが同＋0.29％（年率＋1.18％）、日本経済研究センターが公表した10月のESPフォ
ーキャスト調査（回答期間：2026年9月30日～10月7日、回答者37名）における民間予測値平均が前期
比年率＋0.08％となっている。「みずほGDPナウ」の予測値は、民間コンセンサスよりやや高いもの
の、概ね近い伸びを予測していると言える。 
なお、多くの民間エコノミストが保守的な見方をしている理由の一つには、GDP統計作成上のテク
ニカルな要因がある。4～6月期には、 中東情勢悪化を背景に原油 ・ ナフサ輸入が大幅に減少したほか、
石油化学製品の供給途絶懸念から幅広い企業で在庫積み増しの動きが生じた。やや直感に反するが、
いずれもGDPに対しては押し上げ要因となる。 そのため、4～6月期GDPは前期比＋0.36％ （年率＋1.45％）
とプラス成長になったものの、 成長率が示すほど景気の実態は強くなかったとみるのが自然であろう。
反対に、7～9月期については、4～6月期の反動で輸入増・在庫減の動きが顕在化し、GDPには一定の
下方圧力がかかる可能性がある。みずほ総合研究所が9月に公表したレポート2でも、こうしたテクニ
カル要因を考慮の上、7～9月期GDPは横ばい圏の伸びにとどまると予想している。 
■ 「みずほ GDP ナウ」による予測値下方修正の主因は生産・消費関連指標の悪化 
「みずほGDPナウ」 による7～9月期のGDP（1次速報値） の予測にあたっては10月中旬までに公表さ
れた月次経済指標を使って予測値を4回更新した。図表3では、予測値の更新過程と各月次経済指標の
寄与度を示している3。予測値が下方修正された主因は、8月の鉱工業生産、 消費財出荷指数の低下だ。
特に鉱工業生産指数については、7月末に発生した熊本地震による影響が下押し要因になったとみら
れる。 大手自動車メーカーで工場の操業が停止した影響で自動車が前月比▲6.8％と大幅に減産したほ
か、 電子部品 ・ デバイスも同▲1.9％の減産となった。7月調査時点の生産予測指数は前月比＋3.1％（ 補
図表 2 各機関の実質GDP成長率予測値 図表 3 予測値の改定過程と各経済指標の寄与度

### Page 3

3

正値ベース）と増産が見込まれていたが、大幅に下振れた格好だ。一方、世界的に旺盛なAI・データ
センター関連需要を背景に、半導体製造装置を含む生産用機械などが増産したものの、熊本地震の影
響を打ち返せなかった。消費財出荷指数の低下については、4～5月の耐久財特需が一巡したことで反
動減の動きが生じた。非耐久財の出荷は前月から増加したものの、7月の落ち込みも踏まえると、 基調
としては力強さを欠いていると言えよう。長雨や低温、といった天候不順により生鮮野菜価格が上昇
しているほか、食肉も値上がりが大きく、こうした手に取る機会が多い品目の価格上昇が家計の節約
志向を強め、個人消費の下押し圧力となっているとみられる。9月についても、食品のほか、原油・石
油化学製品等のコスト高の影響で幅広い品目の価格上昇が加速する見込みであり、個人消費は冴えな
い動きが続く可能性がある。 
以上のように、7～9月期成長率は低い伸びに抑えられる公算が大きい。前述のとおり原油・ナフサ
の輸入増、 企業の在庫調整といった動きはGDP統計上、成長率に対してマイナスに寄与する。加えて、
熊本地震による生産減や天候要因による消費支出抑制の動きが下押し要因になる見込みだ。 
次回の 「みずほGDPナウ」については、11月16日に公表される7～9月期GDP1次速報を挟んで、10～
12月期GDPのリアルタイム予測に取り組む。10月分の鉱工業生産や消費活動指数の結果等を踏まえて
推計し、12月中旬頃のレポート発刊を予定している。原油高等を受けた物価上昇圧力の強まりや堅調
なAI・データセンター関連需要など、プラス・マイナス双方の影響が交錯する中、ナウキャストによ
る景気動向の把握はより重要なものとなるだろう。

1 ステップワイズ法により使用データを採択した。詳細は太田ほか（2024）を参照されたい。今後のモデルの予測精度のパフォ
ーマンス評価等を踏まえ、採択するデータについては見直しを行う可能性がある。 
2 詳細については、服部直樹「年率＋1.4％と 1 次速報から小幅に上方修正（4～6 月期2 次QE）」（2026 年9 月8 日）を参照され
たい。 
3 太田他（2024）で示した枠組みと同様、図表 2 の折れ線が各時点における実質GDP 成長率の予測値であり、月次の経済指標が
新たに公表されたり改定されたりすることで予測値が更新される。棒グラフは、予測値の改定幅、すなわち前回予測との差を各
月次経済指標で寄与度分解したもので、寄与度を合計するとモデル予測値の改定幅と一致する。なお、 「みずほGDP ナウ」は各
月次経済指標についても公表前に予測値を計算しており、各指標の寄与度はモデル予測値と実績値の乖離から計算されることか
ら、寄与度の符号は経済指標の変化の方向とは必ずしも一致しない。

Document 026: 2026-11-05_［みずほ経済インサイト］欧州大規模デモが問う財政再建の道_c421a30a

### Page 1

欧州大規模デモが問う財政再建の道 
金融市場が動揺するリスクは当面残存する見通し

○ このところ欧州ではインフレに伴う生活苦や公共サービスの劣化等を背景に各地で大規模抗議活
動が発生している 
○ 大規模デモは緊縮を伴う財政健全化を難しくする要因となる。財政懸念や政情不安は国債利回り
の上昇やユーロ安を招いており、経済への悪影響も懸念される 
○ 来年にかけて、欧州では複数の重要な国政選挙が予定される中、政治・財政を巡る不安が欧州金
融市場を動揺させるリスクは当面残存するだろう

１．欧州で広がる大規模抗議活動 
このところ、欧州では大規模デモが多発している（図表1）。フランスでは、9月以降、教員不足や
学校施設の不備など、教育環境の改善を求める高校生の抗議活動が続いていた。こうした中で、10月
半ばには、物価高への対策や公共サービスの改善を求める市民も加わり、抗議活動は全国規模に拡大
した。 
大規模デモが発生しているのはフランスだけではない。フランスのデモに一部触発される形でベル
図表 1 9 月以降に欧州で発生した主な抗議活動 図表2 EU：生活苦の割合（左）と 
自国が抱える課題（右）

### Page 2

2

ギーやイタリア等でも抗議活動が発生している。また、スペインでは、高齢女性が家賃を支払えずに
強制退去させられたことをきっかけに、住宅不足や住宅価格・家賃高騰等に対する不満が爆発する形
で大規模な抗議活動が発生し、これが遠因となりサンチェス首相は議会の解散に追い込まれた。 
各地で発生する大規模抗議活動の背景には、近年のインフレによる生活苦やこれを受けた政府への
不満がある1。EUにおいて、生活苦を訴える人の割合は低中所得層を中心に2022年以降高まっている
ほか、 自国が直面する課題としてインフレや経済状況を挙げる人の割合も高い （図表2）。これらの要
因が、自国政府に対する信頼感の低迷につながっている（図表3）。 
本稿では、生活改善を求めて各国で広がる抗議活動が経済や財政、金融市場に与える影響について
考察する。 
２．大規模抗議活動は財政健全化を阻む要因に 
一連の抗議活動は、2018年にフランスで発生した黄色いベスト運動を想起させる2。 当時の参加者は、
自動車に常備が義務付けられている黄色い安全ベストを着用し、燃料税の増税や生活費高騰に抗議し
た。購買力の低下や政府への不満が背景にある点は当時と共通している。異なるのは、政府の追加的
な財政支出の余地が、当時と比べてさらに狭まっていることである。 
欧州債務危機が起きた2010年代前半以降、順調に進捗してきた政府債務の削減は、2020年代に入る
と足踏みしはじめた。パンデミック時の大規模給付やウクライナ戦争後のエネルギー高対策など、危
機対応の財政出動が頻発したためだ。黄色いベスト運動が発生した2018年と比べて政府債務が増加し
たEU加盟国は、27カ国中18カ国にのぼる（図表4）。 
加えて、足元では防衛費増額や社会保障費・利払い負担の拡大など、構造的な財政支出圧力も高ま
っている。 各国の次年度予算案を踏まえると、 財政赤字は当面、高止まりする公算が大きい （図表5） 。
安全保障環境の変化やエネルギー価格の高騰を踏まえ、EUは一定の防衛費・エネルギー関連の追加支
出をEU財政ルールの対象外とする柔軟化措置を設けている。 しかし、 当該措置を適用しているドイツ
図表3 EU市民の自国政府への信頼度 図表4 EU加盟国の一般政府債務残高の変化 
（2018年～2025年）

加盟国の3分の2で債務が増加

### Page 3

3

やベルギーでは、特例対象の追加支出を除いてもなお、財政赤字をGDP比3％に抑制するEUの基準を
超過する見込みである。それでもベルギーでは、連立与党間の予算協議が決裂し、首相が辞意を表明
する事態に陥った。 
フランスを起点に広がった抗議活動は、 緊縮を伴う財政健全化の難しさを浮き彫りにしている。2018
年の黄色いベスト運動では、マクロン政権が燃料税増税の撤回や所得支援策の拡充に踏み切ったこと
で、抗議活動が漸く沈静化に向かった。今回は、11月に新たなデモやストライキが計画されるなど、
いまだ抗議活動が収束する見通しは立っていない。各国政府は事態の長期化を回避するため、家計支
援や公共サービス拡充などの対応を迫られる可能性があり、財政拡張圧力が一段と強まることが懸念
される。 
３．政治・財政を巡る不安が欧州金融市場を動揺させるリスクは当面残存 
金融市場は、欧州主要国の財政状況に対する警戒を強めている。 
フランスをはじめとする高債務国では、政治や財政を巡る混乱を嫌気して、10年債利回りが上昇し
ている（図表6）。2010年代の欧州債務危機を受けてECB（欧州中央銀行）は、条件付きで対象国の国
債を無制限に購入可能なOMT（Outright Monetary Transactions）や経済の基礎的条件では正当化されな
い国債市場の混乱が金融政策の波及を阻害する場合に発動可能なTPI （Transmission Protection 
Instrument）などの市場安定化措置3を整備してきた。当時よりセーフティーネットが整備されている
こともあり、足元の国債利回り上昇が欧州債務危機時のような金融危機につながる可能性は現時点で
は低い。 
とはいえ、金利の上昇は景気下押しや、さらなる財政悪化の要因となる。金利上昇幅が大きいフラ
ンスを例に足元の利回り上昇幅を踏まえて試算すると、設備投資・住宅投資が1年後に3％程度減少す
るとの結果が得られる （図表7） 。金利上昇やそれに伴う景気減速は、利払い費の増加や税収の減少を
図表5 主要国の財政収支実績と見通し 図表6 ユーロ圏の高債務国と 
ドイツの10年債利回り

きなかった国。数値は成立前の政府予算案ベース。

### Page 4

4

通じて、財政をさらに悪化させる要因にもなる。 
各国の政情不安や財政への懸念は、ECBの金融政策運営をも左右する可能性がある。足元では、ユ
ーロが主要通貨に対して下落している （図表8） 。 ユーロ安は、 輸入物価の上昇を通じてインフレ率を
押し上げる要因となる。足元のユーロ下落幅を前提に、時系列モデルを用いてユーロ圏のインフレ率
への影響を試算すると、総合インフレ率は0.3％Pt程度、コアインフレ率は0.1％Pt程度押し上げられる
との結果が得られた（図表9）。 ユーロ圏では、イラン情勢の緊迫化に伴うエネルギー価格の高騰を背
景に、直近でもインフレ率が加速している。先行きユーロ安圧力が強い状況が続けば、インフレ率に
一段の上昇圧力がかかる可能性がある。しかし、ECBはインフレ圧力が一段と強まっても、状況によ
っては追加利上げに踏み切りにくくなることが想定される。利上げがさらなる長期金利の上昇、国債
市場の不安定化を招くおそれがあるためだ。このように政情不安や財政懸念は、ECBの金融政策運営
を困難にする要因にもなりかねない。 
各国の財政や政治情勢に対する金融市場の懸念は当面残存する可能性が高い。欧州では、今年11月
にスペイン議会選挙、来年にはフランス大統領選挙やイタリア議会選挙など、重要な国政選挙が相次
いで予定されている。大規模デモの発生に加えて選挙も控える中、各国政府は国民に負担を求める緊
縮的な財政政策を例年以上に打ち出しにくくなっている。また、各国の選挙では極右政党やポピュリ
スト政党の勢力拡大が予想され、選挙後の政治はより一層不安定化するリスクがある。このほか、コ
ロナ禍からの経済復興を目的に設立されたEU復興基金の受け取りが終了することも、 来年以降の財政
懸念を強める要因になるだろう。同基金の配分が手厚かった南欧や中東欧諸国は、自国の財政負担を
抑えながら公共投資などを通じて経済成長を支えてきたが、来年以降はこうした効果が徐々に剥落す
ることになる。

### Page 5

5

以上のように、2020年代に入ってから、コロナ禍におけるサプライチェーン混乱、ロシアのウクラ
イナ侵攻やイラン情勢の緊迫によるエネルギー高が立て続けに起きてインフレが継続する中、欧州各
国で有権者の生活コスト上昇に対する不満が強まっている。一方、これらの緊急事態に対応するため
財政出動を繰り返してきたことに加え、高齢化に伴う社会保障費の増加、地政学リスクの高まりを背
景とした防衛費増強の必要性、利払い費の増大に直面しており、各国の財政余力は著しく低下してい
る。来年にかけて予定されている主要国の選挙では、極右・ポピュリスト政党の勢力拡大が予想され
ており、政治の混乱に拍車がかかる懸念もある。欧州の政治情勢や財政状況に対する不安が来年にか
けて沈静化するとは考えにくく、国債市場や為替市場が動揺するリスクに引き続き留意する必要があ
ろう。

Document 119: 261030_daiwa_MTK

Source PDF: all report pdf/261030_daiwa_MTK.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: MediaTek (MTK) disclosed on 30 October a strong set of 
quarterly results and guidance both beating market expectations, thanks to 
a faster-than-expected ramp in its first AI accelerator project. We walked 
away from today’s call with a firmer view on its AI business - a faster ramp 
for the first project and a broadened customer base for a new project. We 
raise our forecasts and 12M TP, reiterate our Buy (1) call and suggest that 
investors take advantage of the recent stock pullbacks for bargain-hunting.

What’s the impact: Results and guidance all beat. MTK released 3Q26 
net profit of c.TWD28bn, beating our/consensus estimates by 11%/9% 
thanks to a better topline, lower opex ratio and higher non-operating gains 
(table on p.2). 3Q26 revenue finished 6%/4% above our/consensus 
estimates and topped guidance (TWD152-160bn), thanks to an earlier 
ramp of its first AI accelerators on some easing of the substrate supply 
constraint. Guidance calls for 4Q26 revenue of TWD216bn, at the midpoint 
of the range that beat our previous/consensus forecasts by 7%/3%, driven 
by the stronger ramp of the AI accelerator project which will more than 
offset muted consumer demand weakness associated with mobile phones. 
We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 1-6% and expect its AI revenue to reach 
USD16bn in 2027 and USD26bn in 2028, from USD2.7bn this year.

AI appears to be running ahead. At today’s call, management centred 
elaboration on its AI business progress. 1) It raised this year’s initial 
revenue contribution of the first AI accelerators to USD2.5bn for 4Q26, 
above its previous target of USD2bn. 2) While management keeps its AI 
accelerator (ex-HBM) addressable market unchanged at USD80bn for 
2027, it now aims for a 20% market share (previous: 15-20%), meaning 
USD16bn revenue contribution, vs. our previous forecast of USD15bn. 3) It 
expects revenue contribution in 2028 from the second AI customer (not 
from China), which supports our view depicted in our Global Tech report 
issued on 27 August, where we lifted MTK’s 2028 forecasts on the second 
customer ramp. 4) MTK aims to kick off 4Q26 with a new journey for its 
operating margin, expanding all the way into 2027 for more than 20% on 
average, echoing its previous comments that, although the AI accelerator 
business is gross-margin dilutive, it is operating-margin accretive.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we finetune our 12M 
TP for MTK to TWD5,500 (previous: TWD5,200), based on our unchanged 
4-quarter forward PER of 36x. We reiterate our Buy (1) rating with key 
downside risk of any AI accelerator ramps below our expectation.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E EPS are 6-8% above the consensus 
forecasts as we believe the street has yet to factor in its better AI ramp.

30 October 2026

Firmly on track with AI ASIC transformer

➢ 3Q26 results and 4Q26 guidance all beat expectations 
➢ Raise our forecasts on faster-than-expected AI ramps 
➢ Reiterate Buy (1) rating with a higher 12M TP of TWD5,500

Company profile 
Ranked No.5 in the world by 2023 fabless semiconductor revenue, MediaTek (MTK) offers diverse 
products across optical storage, digital home, smartphone, PC, ASIC, automotive and other IoT 
applications. MTK has successfully captured the industry demand cycles from optical disk drives to 
feature phones to smartphones, and now positions itself well as a platform provider to capitalise on the 
next demand cycle of the BigData/AI to help facilitate digital transformation in human lives.

### Page 5

5

MediaTek (2454 TT): 30 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 4 Feb 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
MTK’s Board of Directors (BoD) has 8 members with diverse backgrounds in foundry, IC design
and electronics. Among its BoD, 4 members are independent directors, representing 50% of total
directors. Its Chairman and CEO are not the same person, but its CEO is also one of the
directors. MTK conducts internal annual performance evaluation of the BoD, individual directors
and functional committees.
The board currently includes only one female director out of eight members, which represents
12.5% of the total. The company recognizes this gender imbalance and is actively working
toward a more inclusive and balanced board composition
Capital management 2
MTK’s dividend payout ratios in the past 5 years were above 60% with increasing cash dividend
in the past 3 years in dollar amount. In addition, MTK is committed to paying a special dividend
of TWD16/share over 2021-24 on top of its regular annual dividend. MTK is one of the leading
fabless chipmakers in the world with continued efforts in new product development for diversified
applications; therefore, it will continue to invest in its research & development. We see its
dividend payout as appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 1
MTK’s revenue from related parties was less than 1% of its total revenue in 2024 and transaction
terms were not abnormal compared to other customers. It also had related-party transactions
with King Yuan Electronics Co., Ltd. and King Long Tech (Suzhou) Ltd for IC testing,
experimental services, and manufacturing technology services in 2021. The purchase amount
from related parties was less than 7% of its total cost of goods sold/purchases in 2024. Thus, we
do not see meaningful impact on its business operation.
S Supply chain management 2
MTK has established its "Supplier Code of Conduct", which covers areas such as labour and
human rights, health and safety, environmental protection, code of ethics, and management. It
requires all suppliers to sign a guarantee to comply with this code of conduct. MTK implements
an annual review to continuously track the actual compliance of suppliers. We believe these
measures can help MTK reduce supply chain risk.
E Water & wastewater management 1
As a fabless without manufacturing plants, MTK does not produce much wastewater compared
to manufacturing companies, and thus has relatively limited impact on the environment.
MTK has initiated water conservation methods, including efficient enhancement of using water-
saving apparatuses, installing rainwater recycling tanks and using recycled water in cooling
towers.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
MTK prioritises waste reduction, classification for reuse, adherence to recycling, and reutilisation
to manage waste efficiently and maximise the benefits of recycling. Also, it chooses only
qualified partners for waste disposal and recycling, and also audits the waste processes
regularly to ensure legal disposal of its waste and to fulfil its responsibility in waste management
supervision.
E GHG emissions 1
The main source for MTK’s GHG emissions is externally purchased electricity, which accounts
for over 90% of its total emissions. To enhance energy efficiency and reduce emissions, the
company implemented several measures, including ongoing improvements to data center
efficiency, full adoption of LED lighting in offices, and optimized operation of chiller units.
MediaTek continued advancing its renewable energy strategy and alignment with global climate
initiatives such as SBT and RE100. In early 2025, the Company passed the SBTi review and set
targets based on a 2020 baseline, including a 40% reduction in Scope 1 and 2 emissions by
2030, net zero by 2050, and achieving 100% renewable energy use across global offices
(excluding data centers) by 2030.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

MediaTek (2454 TT): 30 October 2026

Date: 2026-11-05

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following VPEC’s robust 3Q26 earnings release on 30 
October and our subsequent market research, we raise our forecasts and 
12M TP, premised upon: 1) a higher top-line growth, thanks to its faster 
capacity conversion to meet strong demand for AI optical communication 
(OC) devices; and 2) a higher substrate supply consigned by customers, 
helping margin. Our thesis remains intact for VPEC to enjoy strong growth 
from its share gains in the AI OC market, with broadened customer base 
and applications. Given increased jitters on AI bubble and macro issues 
post the US mid-term elections, we upgrade our rating one notch to Buy (1) 
and suggest investors take advantage of market volatility to bargain-hunt.

What’s the impact: Forecast changes. We raise our 2026-27E EPS by 3-
4% to factor in VPEC’s robust 3Q26 results and higher revenue growth in 
4Q26, on our latest market research. This is premised upon: 1) faster-than-
expected capacity conversion from GaAs for mobile to InP for AI OC at its 
MOCVD reactors to mitigate supply constraints and meet more optical 
demand; and 2) increased InP substrate supply consigned by customers, 
helping to increase GM. As such, we expect VPEC’s earnings growth to 
remain strong through 2027, supporting its hefty valuation.

Speeding up capacity conversion to meet more demand. Given a 
limited floor space in its Fab 2, VPEC may accommodate 70+ MOCVD 
reactors in 2027 (currently: 65). Yet, given still-suboptimal utilisation in its 
RF business due to weak consumer demand for smartphones on cost 
inflation, VPEC is accelerating capacity conversion to accommodate strong 
AI OC demand, in order to narrow the supply/demand gap.

Increasing substrate consignment to benefit GM. Due to the InP 
substrate supply constraint and strong AI demand, customers with strong 
purchasing power are increasing substrate consignment to VPEC, adding 
to GM since the consigned materials are excluded from the GM calculation. 
We continue to expect the company to register AI-related revenue, 
including PD, LD and HAMR, with broadened customer base, of TWD1.2-
1.3bn this year, TWD3bn in 2027E and at least TWD4bn in 2028E, from 
TWD550m in 2025 (see our preview note, 8 September).

What we recommend: We raise our 12M TP for VPEC to TWD555 
(previous: TWD535), still based on our 4-quarter-forward PER of 58x. We 
upgrade our rating by one notch to Buy (1), with key downside risk of 
China’s export control inducing any unexpected supply chain hiccups.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E EPS are 14-36% below Bloomberg 
consensus, as we believe the street is too aggressive on VPEC’s AI ramp.

5 November 2026

Upgrading: value resurfaces after jitters rise

➢ Raising forecasts on both higher top-line and margin trajectories 
➢ Intact optical ramp in AI connectivity despite market volatility 
➢ Upgrading our rating to Buy (1) with a higher 12M TP of TWD555

Company profile 
Founded in 1996, Visual Photonics Epitaxy (VPEC) is a veteran epiwafer maker in Taiwan. The 
company started with growing epiwafers for LED applications and then transitioned to microwave 
communications. Strategically moving up the technology verticals, in recent years VPEC has tapped into 
the high-value-add applications of optical sensing & communications for both blended ASP and margin 
accretion.

### Page 5

Visual Photonics Epitaxy (2455 TT): 5 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 5 Nov 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
VPEC's board of directors (BoD) has 11 members, including 3 independent directors,
representing 27% of total directors in 2023, which is slightly higher than the regulatory
requirement but lower than at other semiconductor companies under our coverage. Its chairman
and general manager are not the same person and most of the directors are not executive
management. In addition to the annual internal performance evaluation of the BoD, VPEC also
requests an external evaluation of its BoD at least once every 3 years. We believe this helps
strengthen the functionalities of the BoD despite a relatively low percentage of independent
directors.
Capital management 1
In the past 10 years, the company’s payout ratio has been above 78%. In terms of the dollar
amount, its cash dividends were TWD2.3 per share in 2017-23. We view VPEC has well-
managed dividend policy and see its dividend payout ratio is appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 1 The company does not have any meaningful related-party transactions.
S Supply chain management 2
VPEC has set up a "Supplier Management Procedure (M0047)" and evaluates its suppliers in
terms of environmental management, compliance, and green products. The company audits its
suppliers to ensure a good performance in environmental sustainability, health & safety and
corporate social responsibility.
E Water & wastewater management 2 It has appointed professionals according to regulations to conduct environmental management of air pollution, waste water, waste and hazardous substances. Also, it has introduced the ISO14001 environmental management system and passed the SGS certification in 2002.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
3
VPEC conducts audits on companies that handle the company’s environmentally sensitive waste
disposal to ensure the fulfilment of environmental sustainability and the implementation of
corporate social responsibility.
E GHG emissions 3 It has set a target to reduce its GHG emissions and energy consumption per unit of product by 5% per year and verify greenhouse gas inventories bt ISO 14064 regulations.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

King Yuan Electronics Corp.

Testing business diversification strengthen 
’27 outlook at low-end valuation

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 330.00 TWD | Price: 228.50 TWD

3Q26 results in line; 4Q TPU ramp offsets GPU headwind 
3Q26 sales were +9% QoQ backed by healthy demand from its GPU/CPU customers and 
early mobile inventory builds. With higher depreciation and labor cost moving to COGS 
as new facility production kick off, GM dipped 190bps QoQ to 37.6% in 3Q26 but 
operating leverage lifted OpM 40bps QoQ to 25.8%, taking EPS to NT$2.06. 4Q26 sales 
is guided +7-9% QoQ, in line with our preview, due to slower GPU ramp from continued 
HBM4 yield issue at Hynix partially offsetting strength in AI ASICs (Google’s v8t/i TPUs) 
and CPUs (Nvidia Vera, Google Axion and Microsoft Cobalt). Despite better business mix, 
the rising cost and slower UTR improvement should keep GMs stable at 36-37% in 4Q. 
Solid position into 2027 across chipset and wafer testing 
KYEC’s ’26 sales is now on track to grow 34% YoY and could further accelerate in ’27 at 
+53% YoY from a diversifying application base across GPU, CPU, networking switches and 
ASICs. It is also in a strong position to capture its foundry partner’s chip probing 
outsourcing demand in addition to higher testing unit/dollar content from its existing 
direct customer business. To support expansion across wafer sorting, chip testing and 
system level testing, the company is accelerating its clean room construction in Taiwan 
and has announced it will grow its testing footprint in the US. While its ’27 capex could 
stay at elevated levels, we think the investment mix shifting toward facility should keep its 
depreciation under control. This along with capex-light tool consignment business model 
could drive its GMs to 40%+ in 1H27 and keep profitability resilient through the cycles. 
Recent pullback to low-end valuation: particularly attractive 
We keep EPS for ‘26/27/28E largely unchanged at NT$7.87/NT$15.36/NT$19.18, and 
reiterate our Buy rating with PO of NT$330 still based on 18x 2H27-1H28E P/E (vs. its 
10-25x P/E range). Following the AI sentiment drag, the stock is trading at 15x/11x 
‘27/28E P/E, attractive as the business downside is protected by its leadership in scale 
and execution for AI chipset testing even if AI investment moderates in ‘28.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Founded in 1987 and headquartered in Hsinchu, Taiwan, 
King Yuan Electronics Corp. (KYEC) is a niche Outsourced 
Semiconductor Assembly and Test (OSAT) company 
focused on IC testing services, with market shares of 3% in 
OSAT (IC packaging+testing, number 7 vendor) and 14% in 
IC testing (number 2 vendor) globally. Its capacity is mainly 
located in Greater China. In addition to IC testing, it has 
c.20% revenue exposure to IC packaging, mainly at its 
Chinese subsidiary and mainly for memory products.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on KYEC, in view of undervalued 
upside from smartphone business for both near-term and 
longer-term, structural opportunities in AI areas led by GPU, 
as well as the solid GPM/ROE expansion toward into 2025.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
King Yuan Electronics Corp. (KYUFF) 
We value KYEC at NT$330 per share, based on 18x 2H27-1H28E P/E, which is close to 
its historical average since 2010. In our view, P/E valuation is appropriate for KYEC given 
its strong growth in earnings over the past few years, future growth potentials with 
upside in total addressable market (TAM) and market share, and enhanced cash position. 
We think 18x P/E, which is above the average P/E level of global OSAT peers, is 
undemanding, in view of its better ROE profile and 2-3% dividend yield.

Downside risks are 
1) faster-than-expected pick-up by testing peers in high-end AI area, 
2) greater-than-expected slowdown in global smartphone/consumer electronics demand, 
3) potential asset disposal caused by strategy shift amid the ongoing decoupling in the 
semis supply chain.

Nan Ya PCB  | Asia Pacific
In-line 3Q26 gross margin
3Q26 results:
• 3Q revenue was NT$16.4bn (+21% q/q, +49% y/y).
• GM reached 29.3% (+460bps q/q, +1,970bps y/y), slightly above our estimate
but in line with the Street, driven by higher ABF (low 30s  %) and BT GM
(high 20s  %).  PCB remains  loss-making on the gross margin level.
• OP was NT$4.3bn (+49% q/q, +597% y/y), 1% above our estimate and the
Street, for OpM of 26.4% (+500bps q/q, +2,070bps y/y).

• However, non-op income was below our estimate and the Street. Tax rate of
~26% was again higher than our estimate at 16% and the Street at 19%.
• Thus, NP came in at NT$3.3bn (+46% q/q, +355% y/y), 13% below our
estimate/8% below the Street, for EPS of  NT$5.10.

Key Takeaways
NYPCB's 3Q GM is  largely in line with both buy-side and sell-side estimates.
The 500bps sequential improvement in OpM looks positive to us. Similar to

Unimicron's 3Q results, we view NYPCB's slight miss as an expectation
mismatch...
...rather than reflecting any structural concerns.
We also expect NYPCB to gain share for ABF substrates for AMD's server CPUs,
as one Japanese competitor reallocates more capacity to another CPU customer.
We remain constructive on all ABF substrate stocks in our coverage.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 14%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity
Nan Ya PCB (8046.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate

value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand
n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.

CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall, creating headwinds for ABF substrate demand and pricing
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Document 181: 3006晶豪科 宏遠

Source PDF: all report pdf/3006晶豪科 宏遠.pdf

### Page 1

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

1 
3006 晶豪科(ESMT)

報告日期： 2026/11/03 研 究 員：王蕎程  (Joe Wang)  
壹、訪談／法說內容 
重點摘要: 
 記憶體漲價帶動獲利： 3Q26 營收 221.50 億元， QoQ 
+58.83% ，毛 利 率77.44%；預 估4Q26 營收 239.22 億元 ，QoQ 
+8.0% ，稅後 EPS 39.50 元。  
 成熟記憶體供給吃緊： DDR2 、DDR3 合計營收占比逾五成，
受惠同業減供及客戶轉單，加上高階、商務應用需求暢旺，支
撐產品價格維持高檔。  
 預估 2026 ／2027 年稅後 EPS 102.33 ／159.38 元：受惠合約
價格調升及低基期，預估全年營收分別為 672.85 ／1,019.90
億元， YoY +361.6% ／+51.6% 。

一. 3Q26 稅後 EPS 36.79 元，漲價帶動毛利率升  
晶豪科主要從事記憶體 IC 設計，產品涵蓋 DRAM、Flash、MCP 、
eMCP 及 eMMC ，今年記憶體產品營收比重約 95% 。受惠記憶體
供給吃緊、合約價格逐季調升， 3Q26 營收 221.50 億元， QoQ 
+58.83% 、YoY +519.22% ；毛利率 77.44% ，較上季增加 8.74 個
百分點；營業利益 131.50 億元， QoQ +83.33% ，營業利益率
59.37% ； 稅後純益107.59 億元 ，QoQ +82.20% ，稅 後EPS 36.79
元。營收及獲利成長主要來自產品價格上漲，出貨量受限於合作
晶圓廠產能，增幅相對有限。

二、 DDR2 、DDR3 營收占比五成，供給收縮支撐價格  
隨記憶體大廠將資源轉向 DDR4 、DDR5 及 HBM ，成熟記憶體供
給減少，缺貨情況逐步擴及 DDR3 、DDR2 及 Flash 。公司表示，
國內同業停止或減少供應 DDR2 、DDR3 ，帶動客戶轉單，第三季
兩項產品合計營收占比已超過 50% 。需求方面， PC、NB 及手機
因應 AI 應用提高記憶體搭載容量，加上 AI 基礎架構、周邊設備
與邊緣運算需求增加，有助帶動非 HBM 記憶體需求。儘管部分消
費性客戶因價格漲幅較大而觀望，高階及商務應用拉貨仍暢旺，
支撐公司接單表現。

三、 4Q26 預估營收季增 8%，aiPIM 布局邊緣 AI 應用  
展望第四季，公司表示產品價格將高於第三季，出貨量則預期與
第三季相近。考量合約價格續升，預估 4Q26 營收 239.22 億元，

四、預估 2026 ／2027 年稅後 EPS 102.33 ／159.38 元 
預估 2026 全年營收 672.85 億元 ，YoY +361.6% ；營 業 毛 利489.51
億元，毛利率 72.75% ；營業利益 370.17 億元，稅後純益 305.07
億元，稅後 EPS 102.33 元。展望 2027 年，受惠成熟記憶體供給
受限、高階及商務應用需求支撐，預期產品價格維持高檔，搭配
2026 上半年較低基期，帶動全年營運成長；惟考量代工及封測成
本逐步反映，毛利率將持平。預計 2027 年營收 1,019.90 億元，
YoY +51.6% ；營業毛利 764.21 億元，毛利率 74.93% ；營業利益
588.51 億元，稅後純益 475.16 億元， YoY +55.8% ，稅後 EPS 
159.38 元。

五. 投資建議買進，目標價 320 元 
考量 AI 趨勢下帶動原廠將產能轉做 HBM，以 及DDR 4 轉入 DDR 
5，帶動利基型記憶體市場正向效益，記憶體供不應求的現象持
續，帶動價格持續大幅上升，使晶豪科營收獲利自 4Q25 開始明
顯跳升， 2027 年全年供需缺口無解，價格上升趨勢趨緩但仍有上
行空間，帶動晶豪科獲利維持高檔，因此，投資建議買進，目標
價 320 元(2X2027 年稅後 EPS) 。

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
晶豪科 (3006) 成立於 1998 年 ， 公司主要以提供各類型記憶體產品
及技術為主。目前記憶體佔營收比重約 90% ，Audio 佔 10% ，其
中特定型 DRAM 產品廣泛應用於 PC 週邊 、 資訊家電 、 及消費性、
通訊等系統。公司已成功建立各種容量及介面規格的特定型
DRAM 產品線 (包括 SDRAM 、DDR I/II/III 及 PSRAM 、低耗電之
Mobile DRAM 等)；在快閃記憶體方面，亦已完成多種容量及介面
類型的 NOR Flash 及 NAND Flash 的開發，可滿足各種特定應用
系統的需求。另外，由於系統級封裝 (SiP) 之需求快速增加，晶豪
科技也已經成功開發上述各項產品所需之「良品晶粒」
(Known-Good-Die;KGD) 產品及多晶片模組封裝 (MCP) 的解決方
案，以滿足客戶的各類需求。尤於晶豪科記憶體產品大部份以利
基型記憶體為主，受標準型記憶體價格波動干擾程度相對較低，
有助維持穩定毛利率。

二.營運概況及展望

1、IoT 應用持續成長，為重要成長動能 
5G 於 2H19 正式商轉，隨著基礎建設逐漸完善，連網速度及穩定
度成熟， 2021 年進入大幅應用階段，除了 5G 智慧型手機需求量
將從 2020 年的 2～2.5 億支倍數成長至 4.5 ～5 億支之外，因 5G
高頻、高速、廣連結的特色，連網速度將較 4G 時代大幅提升 10
～100 倍，將推升物聯網 (IoT) 應用快速成長，預估 5G 同時連網
裝置可達 600 ～800 億個，較 4G 時代的 100 億個大幅成長。看好
未來物聯網市場將蓬勃發展 ， 晶豪科於2020 年新成立物聯網解決
方案部門，提供客戶 RF、Wireless 的 SoC IC 及模組提供低功率
網際網路進行裝置連結，目前使用 90nm 製程節點，未來將進入
到 55nm 。2020 年新成立的物聯網解決方案部門站營收比重僅
<0.5% ，但因全球物聯網發展將因 5G 建設而蓬勃發展，預估繼
2022 年逆勢成長後， 2023 年可望延續此一成長動能。

IOT 為晶豪科近年延伸觸角的重要產品 。 IOT 將建構於 5G 佈建的
完整性， 2020 年為 5G 建置元年， 2021 年將加速建置，加速 IOT
產業發展。由於大部份 IOT 產品僅需要 DDR 3 規格即可，且全球
主要記憶體廠均優先佈局以 Server 使用的 DDR 5 為主，因此，
預估未來 DDR 3 將保持穩定的需求量。晶豪科將以無線模組切入
中國智慧電表領域，及其他各類相關 IOT 產品，為未來營運成長
重要動能。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一. 3Q26 稅後 EPS 36.79 元，漲價帶動毛利率升 
展望第四季，公司表示產品價格高於第三季，出貨量則預期與第
三季相近 。 考量合約價格續升 ， 預估4Q26 營收 239.22 億元 ，QoQ 
+8.0%；毛 利 率78.44%， 營業利益144.40 億元 ， 稅後純益117.76
億元， EPS 39.50 元。惟晶圓代工及封裝測試成本逐步增加，後
續仍須關注售價調升與成本上漲的相對幅度。新產品方面，公司
針對邊緣 AI 運算開發 aiPIM 加速器記憶體，第三季已完成
tape-out ，後續關注晶片驗證、客戶導入及量產進度，短期營運仍
以既有記憶體產品為主要動能。。

Issue & News 
SK하이닉스  
(000660) 
 소음보다 방향성에 
류형근

hyungkeun.ryu@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 3,200,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.09.23) 
1,862,000 
반도체업종

◼ 최근 시장은 2가지 Risk를 제기. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 
◼ 현 주가는 보다 강인해지는 Fundamental의 가치를 미반영한 수준. 
◼ 비중 확대 전략 유효. 목표주가와 매수의견 유지.

투자전략: 업황을 대변하지 못하고 있는 주가 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 현 주가는 업황의 강세를 충분히 반영하지 못하
고 있다고 생각. 1) 메모리반도체 사이클의 추가 확장 (구매 경쟁 심화에 따른 
단기 가격 상승 폭 확대, 기대치를 상회할 4Q26 DRAM 판매량), 2) 3Q26 실
적발표에서 보다 구체화될 주주환원 정책의 방향성에 보다 집중할 필요. 
시장에서 제기하는 Risk가 있다면, 2가지. 1) 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈, 
2) Solidigm IPO와 해외 공장 투자에 따른 경계심리 확대가 그 대상. 당사는 
해당 Risk가 Fundamental을 저해할 것으로 보고 있지 않으며, 단기 노이즈보
다는 사이클 확장의 선순환에 보다 주목할 필요가 있다고 생각. 
1. HBM4 품질 이슈, 과도한 우려는 기우 
3Q26부터 HBM4 출하가 본격화될 것이라는 기존 입장을 유지. HBM4 비중
은 3Q26 전체 판매량의 40%, 2027년 연간 50% 중반까지 상승할 것. 
일각에서는 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈로 사업 성과가 기대치를 하회할 
수 있다는 우려를 표출. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 후공정 단에서 문제가 일
부 발생했을 수 있으나, 해결 불가능한 Risk라 보기 어렵고, 불량에 따른 배
상금을 논할 단계도 아니라는 판단. 
2. 해외 공장 투자, 실보다 득이 크다 
언론보도에 따르면, SK하이닉스는 해외 공장 투자와 Solidigm IPO를 추진 중. 
해당 결정이 현실화될 경우, 시장은 단기적으로 이를 부정적으로 받아들일 
가능성이 존재. 다만, 결과적으로, 실보다 득이 큰 선택이 될 것이라 생각. 
- 해외 투자가 현실화되더라도, 실질 생산 기여 시점은 2028년 이후일 가능
성이 높음. 미국의 건설 경기와 공장 구축의 세부 단계 등을 감안할 필요. 
- 해외 투자 결정이 관세 Risk를 축소시킬 수 있는 변화라는 점도 감안할 필
요. “불확실성 해소”는 Upcycle의 중반부에서 반도체 주식에 대한 좋은 투자
포인트가 되어왔고, 이번에도 다르지 않을 것이라는 판단.

Document 247: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458.pdf

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
미국 록빌·5공장 가세로 3분기 기대치 부합 전망 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 연결 매출액은 1조 3,637억 원(+8.4% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,092억 원(-3.8% YoY, 영업이익률 44.7%)으로 시장 기대치에 부합할 
전망이다. 5월 파업에 따른 생산 차질에도 1~4공장(총 60.4만 리터)의 풀가동과 
신규 공장의 매출 기여가 외형 성장을 뒷받침할 것으로 예상한다. 미국 록빌 
공장(6만 리터)은 3분기부터 매출 기여가 본격화되고, 5공장(18만 리터)도 
인증용(PPQ) 배치 생산을 통해 매출이 확대될 전망이다. 2분기에는 미국 공장·5공장 
관련 비용이 선반영됐으나, 3분기에는 매출 증가에 따른 고정비 부담 완화로 
영업이익률이 전분기 대비 0.3%p 상승할 것으로 예상한다. 
항체 중심 CDMO에서 펩타이드로 확장 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 신규 수주는 약 1.2조 원으로 전년 수주액의 약 20% 
수준에 그치고 있으나, 연말부터 내년까지 수주 모멘텀은 점차 강화될 것으로 
판단한다. 2025년 하반기 이후 미국의 의약품 관세 불확실성이 완화되며 글로벌 
제약사와의 논의가 늘어나고 있어, 신규 수주 계약으로 이어질 것으로 기대된다. 
11월 유상증자 납입이 마무리되면 6공장(18만 리터) 착공과 스위스 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 본격화되며 중장기 성장 기대감도 높아질 전망이다. 특히 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 연내 완료되면 2027년부터 연결 실적에 반영되는 동시에, 항체 의약품 
중심의 CDMO 사업을 GLP-1 등 펩타이드로 확장하는 계기가 될 전망이다. 
목표주가 200만 원으로 하향, 수주 회복이 관건 
삼성바이오로직스의 목표주가를 209만 원에서 200만 원으로 하향한다. 유상증자 
신주 발행에 따른 주당가치 4.7% 희석 효과를 반영했다. 폴리펩타이드 인수 효과는 
거래완료 이후 실적 추정치에 반영할 예정이다. 노조 파업 불확실성이 주가에 
부담으로 작용할 수 있어 신규 수주 모멘텀의 회복이 필요한 시점이다. 
목표주가 (하향) 2,000,000원  
현재가 (10/6) 1,310,000원

동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

Document 260: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

(373220) 
LG 에너지솔루션 
2026.10.12

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

보상금 제외해도 호실적 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 530,000원(상향) 
 현재주가 401,000원(10/8)

 [투자의견] 당사는 LG에너지솔루션에 대해 실적 추정치 상향을 반영하여 목
표주가를 530,000원으로 13% 상향하고, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 
3Q26부터 ESS 물량 증가에 따른 가동률 상승 효과가 본격적으로 실적에 
반영되기 시작했으며, 4Q26에도 AMPC 제외 흑자 기조를 이어갈 전망이
다. 전사 가동률은 3분기 65%까지 상승한 것으로 파악되며, 이에 따른 펀
더멘털 개선이 본격화되고 있다고 판단한다. 
 [3Q26 잠정 실적 Review] 매출액 9.6조원(+28%qoq, +59%yoy), 영업이
익 7,560억원(+567%qoq, +26%yoy)을 기록하며 당사 추정을 큰 폭으로 
상회했다. 북미 생산보조금(AMPC)을 제외한 매출액은 9.2조원, 영업이익은 
3,391억원(영업이익률 3.7%)으로, 2 분기(-1,277억원, -1.7%) 대비 흑자 
전환에 성공했다. ① 원통형은 테슬라향 물량 호조에 힘입어 영업이익 
2,010억원(영업이익률 8.1%)을 달성한 것으로 추정된다. ② EV는 유럽 폴
란드 공장의 가동률 상승과 GM 얼티엄의 8월 가동 개시로 매출이 증가했
다. 여기에 완성차 업체 보상금 3,000억원이 반영되며 영업이익 1,090억
원(영업이익률 2.5%)을 기록한 것으로 추정된다. ③ ESS는 매출액이 50% 
이상 급증하며 실적 서프라이즈를 견인했다. AMPC 포함 영업이익은 4,460
억원(영업이익률 16.0%)으로 추정된다. 2 분기 매출 성장률 +17%qoq에 
이어 3분기 성장세가 더욱 가팔라졌다는 점이 긍정적이다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 8.0조원(-17%qoq, +30%yoy), 영업이익 6,790
억원(-10%qoq, 흑전yoy)으로 추정한다. 4 분기 실적은 ESS 수익성 개선 
속도와 완성차 업체 보상금 반영 규모에 따라 변동할 전망이다. 동사는 기존 
가이던스를 통해 하반기 북미 ESS 생산량이 상반기 대비 2배 증가할 것으
로 제시한 바 있다. 3분기 ESS는 AMPC를 제외하더라도 양호한 수익성을 
기록한 것으로 추정된다. 향후 팩 생산 병목 해소와 북미 공장 가동 안정화
에 따른 고정비 부담 완화가 이어지며 추가적인 손익 개선이 가능할 전망이
다. EV 역시 GM 얼티엄 생산 확대와 유럽 공장 가동률 상승이 지속되며 
AMPC 제외 적자 규모를 점진적으로 축소할 것으로 예상한다. 
 [투자포인트] 9월 미시간 랜싱 공장의 개소식이 개최됐다. 랜싱 공장은 당초 
EV용 파우치 배터리 생산거점으로 계획됐으나 ESS 중심으로 역할이 재편
됐으며, 내년에는 10GWh 이상의 각형 LFP 생산라인이 추가될 예정이다. 
2027년에는 신규 대규모 증설보다 기존 북미 생산거점의 가동률 상승과 
ESS 생산량 확대를 통한 수익성 개선이 핵심이 될 전망이다. 북미 전력망 
접속 지연과 AI 데이터센터의 부하 변동성 확대에 따라 Stand-alone ESS, 
BTM, UPS, BBU 수요가 증가하면서 신규 수주도 지속 확대될 것으로 예상
한다. 특히 동사의 Non-PFE 요건 충족과 SI· O&M을 아우르는 End-to-
End 솔루션 역량이 북미 ESS 시장에서 경쟁력으로 작용할 전망이다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -2.62 điểm tương ứng -0.15%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Dệt may, Công nghệ và Máy móc là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất trong 
tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -13,987 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã HDB, PNJ và VJC; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-30 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,267 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -2,363 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 3,726 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -2,702 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam trải qua một tuần đi ngang giằng co, giảm nhẹ và đóng cửa ở 1.735 điểm 
với thanh khoản ở mức thấp, và nhà đầu tư nước ngoài bán ròng cả 5 phiên trong tuần. 
Thông tin vĩ mô tích cực với tăng trưởng GDP quý 3 đạt mức gần 10% là điểm sáng trong 
tuần qua, tuy đã không đủ nâng đỡ thị trường chung. Thay vào đó, tâm lý thị trường thận 
trọng trước số liệu lạm phát ở mức cao, đợt tăng giá xăng dầu trong nước và mặt bằng lãi 
suất trong nước chưa có dấu hiệu hạ nhiệt. Diễn biến tăng của giá dầu thế giới, và lợi suất 
trái phiếu Chính phủ Mỹ cũng là các yếu tố gây áp lực lên thị trường chung. Tâm lý thận 
trọng còn thể hiện qua áp lực bán giải chấp ở một số cổ phiếu riêng lẻ như PNJ, SHB..., dù 
một vài nhóm trụ như dầu khí, tiêu dùng có thời điểm đóng vai trò nâng đỡ chỉ số.

Trần Đức Anh 
Giám đốc Kinh tế Vĩ mô & Chiến lược thị trường 
anhtd@kbsec.com.vn 
07/10/2026

Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
Tích lũy trong vùng nhiễu động

Chúng tôi dự báo vùng điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm 2026 
xuống 1,800 đi ểm, giảm mạnh từ mức 1,950 đi ểm đưa ra trong báo cáo gần nhất. 
Vùng điểm này tương ứng:

- Tăng trưởng EPS 2026 so với cùng kỳ của thị trường chung ở mức 33% 
(tăng mạnh so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ yếu nhờ kỳ vọng 
vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và lợi nhuận tăng 
trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt động kinh doanh).

- Mức P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 13.2 (giảm 
nhẹ so với mức 13.5 thời điểm hiện tại - phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố 
rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là mức định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm 
gần nhất ở 15.1. Nếu loại bỏ yếu tố đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup, chỉ số P/E 
điều chỉnh hiện đã rơi xuống 10.9, thấp hơn các đáy gần nhất năm 2020, 
2022 và 2025. Trong bối cảnh kinh tế Việt Nam duy trì tốc độ tăng trưởng 
cao, lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết liên tục bứt phá, vùng định giá 
này mở ra cơ hội đầu tư trung/dài hạn. Chúng tôi cho rằng ngay khi các rủi 
ro vĩ mô quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran), và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn 
đề lãi suất) hạ nhiệt, thị trường chung sẽ có cơ hội tái định giá lên mức cao 
hơn đáng kể so với thời điểm hiện tại. Dù vậy, khác với báo cáo gần nhất 
vào tháng 7, ở báo cáo này, chúng tôi đẩy lui thời điểm các yếu tố rủi ro vĩ 
mô hạ nhiệt sang năm 2027, đồng nghĩa với quan điểm thận trọng hơn về 
thị trường cho quý 4 2026.

Cụ thể, đối với vấn đề lãi suất, áp lực lạm phát lớn dần, đi kèm nhu cầu vốn cao phục 
vụ hoạt động đầu tư cơ sở hạ tầng sẽ khiến mặt bằng lãi suất duy trì ở mức cao cho 
phần còn lại của năm 2026 (dù thanh khoản hệ thống ghi nhận sự cải thiện đáng kể 
trong 1 quý gần đây). Đối với vấn đề xung đột Trung Đông, các cuộc đàm phán vẫn 
đang bế tắc và ít có kh ả năng dòng ch ảy dầu mỏ tại khu vực này được nối lại ngay 
trong năm nay.

Chủ đề đầu tư chúng tôi l ựa chọn cho 4Q2026 là đ ầu tư công, chính sách ti ền tệ hỗ 
trợ, và xung đột Iran.

Xem đầy đủ tại đây.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành mẫu nến Doji trên khung đồ thị tuần sau hai tuần giảm điểm liên tiếp, 
cho thấy áp lực bán đã phần nào được kiểm soát. Trên khung ngày, nỗ lực hồi phục đầu 
tuần gặp lực cản tại đường SMA50 khiến VNINDEX quay đầu giảm điểm, tiếp tục chớm phá 
đáy ngắn hạn; tuy nhiên vùng hỗ trợ gần đã tạo hiệu ứng thu hút lực cầu bắt đáy, giúp chỉ 
số thu hẹp đà giảm trong phiên cuối tuần. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh khá tiêu cực từ vùng đỉnh 
đầu tháng 9, VNINDEX đang bước vào giai đoạn tìm kiếm điểm cân bằng trở lại và diễn biến 
giằng co điều chỉnh có thể còn kéo dài thêm trong các phiên kế tiếp. Tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn tính từ vùng đáy cuối tháng 7 vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ 
vọng sẽ có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại tại điểm đỡ gần quanh 1710 (đây cũng là chốt chặn cần 
giữ để bảo vệ được xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn). 
Kháng cự xa: 1820 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1775 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1710 (+-10)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Document 399: Rates Spark_ Treasury heat from tech flight - but for how long_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Rates Spark_ Treasury heat from tech flight - but for how long_.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

A creeping move lower in equities would eventually be ignored
by Treasuries. But a large dump in equities would not. Treasuries
are so far very much in vogue. In Europe, bond spreads are
widening again as we head into another weekend of street
unrest. Friday sees US jobs data competing for the spotlight,
although Thursday's low claims were broadly ignored

Padhraic Garvey, CFA, Benjamin Schroeder and Michiel Tukker
AI jitters in equity markets has led to the UST 10yr yield dropping by about
50bp from recent highs
A creeping move lower in equities would
eventually be ignored by Treasuries. But a
large dump in equities would not
The US 10yr yield is now down some 50bp from its recent highs.
The drivers have been real money value hunters (mostly domestic,
but also including international players), some fast money longs in
Treasuries versus payers in SOFR in a short swap spread trade, and
ﬁnally a risk-oﬀ tone in the AI space that's been counteracted by
safety ﬂows into Treasuries. The latter has been the dominant

### Page 2

driver of the last 10-15bp, which has taken the 10yr yield down to
the low 4.80s%. Should the AI rout intensify, it's tough to see long-
end yields doing anything other than trekking still lower in
sympathy.
To what extent is this a US election-driven outcome? We could paint
a narrative in which electorate support for fewer data centres and
greater scrutiny of the AI space is a factor. To an extent, it is. But the
bigger picture sees AI valuations generally under pressure. How

long this persists is tough to call, but suﬃce it to say that the
technical picture does not look great for the coming few weeks at
least. The 4.65% area for the 10yr yield is where we could settle
should the risk-oﬀ tone intensify.
That said, the market has conveniently ignored the elevated
inﬂation environment, the largesse in ﬁscal deﬁcits and the AI
growth boost, which remains ongoing. Thursday saw another very
low jobless claims outcome, and Friday is liable to see the

unemployment rate reconﬁrmed at 4.2% (very low). At some point,
Treasuries will refocus on fundamentals. A creeping move lower in
equities would eventually be ignored by Treasuries. But a large
dump in equities would not; that's where the fall in yields becomes
an even larger lurch lower. We're not calling for it, but we're
watching carefully.
AI jitters take over
The bullish momentum in rates continued, but the driving force
behind it changed. While Thursday’s early session was still marked

by an extension of post-election positive risk sentiment, sentiment
later soured on AI jitters. The VIX index – the equity market’s fear
gauge – jumped to the highest since March as tech stocks came
under increasing pressure.
In bond markets, souring sentiment made itself felt in rewidening
spreads. The 10y OAT/Bund spread ended its brief recovery below
150bp. To be fair, the market also found itself confronted with more
headlines around protests in France, highlighting the political

challenge the country faces trying to consolidate its ﬁnances.

### Page 3

Markets will be looking to the US jobs data to inform their Fed view.
However, the data thus so has kept us well within the low-hire / low-
ﬁre narrative, so it would probably take a larger surprise to impact
the market and take the eyes oﬀ the renewed AI concerns.
Bank of England opens door to hawkish
repricing
The Bank of England hiked as expected, but revised inﬂation
forecasts add additional upside risk to rates. Markets now price in a
50% chance that the next hike will be in December, and then at

least one more hike later in 2027. Every committee member agreed
to hike rates, which means even the doves are on board. This lowers
the bar for future hikes and could add to an already volatile rates
environment. Any upside inﬂation surprises or another surge in oil
or gas prices could quickly turn the narrative more hawkish.
Having said that, the sterling curve still seems too steep to us,
especially when compared to euro and dollar curves. The price

action today also shows that longer-dated rates struggled to move
higher – rates up to 2Y rose, but the 10Y swap rate ended lower on
the day. Part of this suggests markets are front-loading more hikes,
but are turning more sceptical about the BoE's terminal rate. We,
too, struggle to see the BoE end the hiking cycle around 4.5%.

The highlight of the day is the oﬃcial US jobs report. The

Musalem and Bowman are also scheduled to speak.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
SEPTEMBER CPI PREVIEW
Setting the pace for the run into year end
19 October 2026
Key points
• September CPI to rise 0.62%mth 
with the annual pace lifting to 4.1%yr 
from 4.0%yr. The seasonally adjusted 
CPI lifts 0.5%mth, a step down from 
the 0.7% increase in August.  
• September Trimmed Mean (TM) is 
set to lift 0.26%mth holding the 
annual pace steady at 3.6%yr. 
• As we noted in our review of the 
August CPI, our September quarter 
CPI estimate is unchanged at 1.3%qtr 
while the TM remained 0.9%qtr.  
• Auto fuel surged ahead of our 
expectations so along with signs of 
increase pass-through we lifted our 
December quarter CPI to 1.3%qtr/3.9%yr 
and the TM estimate to 0.9%qtr/3.5%yr. 
• This is a 0.8ppt increase to the CPI 
estimate, and a 0.2ppt increase to the 
TM estimate, highlighting inflation 
momentum meaningfully above 
the RBA’s current expectations.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
August inflation came in soft...
Inflation moderated in August following July’s strong 
outcome. The CPI rose 0.4% in the month, in line with our 
expectation but slightly below market consensus of 0.5%, 
taking the annual rate to 4.0%. On a seasonally adjusted 
basis, the CPI increased 0.7%mth, underscoring the impact of 
seasonal discounting in August and the start of the financial 
year repricing in July.
More closely watched are the measures of underlying inflation 
including the Trimmed Mean (TM) and market services. 
The TM rose 0.2% in August, in line with our expectations 
but again softer than the market, leaving the annual pace 
unchanged at 3.6% for a third consecutive month. Meanwhile, 
market services excluding volatiles & holiday travel increased 
0.2%mth/4.1%yr. Notable strength (three-month annualised, 
seasonally adjusted) was seen in sports participation (10.9%), 
clothing repair (7.0%), restaurant meals (6.8%) and other 
household services (5.2%). For full analysis of the August CPI, 
see “August CPI – Came in on our expectations ”.
The expenditure-weighted share of the CPI basket 
experiencing positive price shocks eased to 11% in August 
from 15% in July, while the share experiencing negative price 
shocks declined from 17% to just below 13%. The majority of 
the positive shocks were concentrated in consumer durables 
On a three-month moving average basis, the net momentum 
share (positive minus negative) rose to –4.5% from –7.7% 
in July and a trough of –15.8% in May, suggesting the 
disinflationary impulse may be nearing exhaustion. 
... with September due for a moderate pick-up
Looking ahead to September, early indicators painted a 
mixed picture. The NAB business survey showed retail price 
growth slowing to 0.9%qtr from 1.1%qtr in August, with 
labour cost growth easing to 1.7%qtr from 1.8%qtr, while 
purchase cost growth edged up to 2.4%qtr from 2.3%qtr. 
Nevertheless, all measures remain well above their long-run 
averages while the gap between purchase costs and final 
prices continues to widen, indicating businesses are facing 
margin compression (see here for further detail). Meanwhile, 
consumers’ year-ahead inflation expectations held steady 
at 4.9% in September (easing slightly at 2dp) but surged to 
5.3% in October likely reflecting higher fuel prices, with retail 
petrol and diesel averaging $2.20/l (+8.2%mth) and $2.68/l 
(+7.7%mth). We do believe the auto fuel prices are likely to see 
a near term peak in October.  Meanwhile, global shipping costs 
have also surged recently amid renewed Middle East tensions.
Sep quarter unrevised but Dec upgraded significantly
Justin Smirk, Senior Economist
Neha Sharma, Economist

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The September CPI is expected to rise 0.6% (0.62% to 2dp)
with the annual pace lifting to 4.1%yr. Our seasonally adjusted
estimate increases 0.5% (0.48% to 2dp). For the monthly
TM, we expect a 0.3% (0.26% to 2dp) lift, holding the annual

pace steady at 3.6%. On a 3m/3m basis, the TM is tracking
a 1.0% gain in September (noting that while this measure is
conceptually different to the quarterly TM it still serves as a
useful guide). For market services excluding volatiles & holiday
travel we have pencilled in another 0.2% rise, slowing the
annual pace slightly to 4.0% (a nine-month low).
The largest contributors are recreation & culture (+0.27ppt),
driven wholly by holiday travel & accommodation. Transport

(+0.23ppt) makes a solid contribution, with gains in fuel
(+0.26ppt) partly offset by motor vehicles (–0.02ppt). Housing
also contributes (+0.13ppt), with rents and new dwellings
each adding 0.02ppt while property rates add 0.08ppt.
The key detractors in the month are food & non-alcoholic
beverages (–0.08ppt) and garments (–0.04ppt).
Key details for the month of September
Food & non-alcoholic beverages are expected to fall
0.5%mth, easing the annual pace to 2.8%yr. The weakness is

concentrated in a 3.7%mth decline in fruit & vegetables, while
non-alcoholic beverages are also expected to fall 1.5%mth.
Meals out & takeaway is forecast to rise 0.1%mth, easing the
annual rate to 3.9%.
Housing is expected to rise 0.6% in September, slowing the
annual pace to 5.5%yr. Component-wise:
• Rents are expected to rise 0.3%mth and 3.7%yr. CPI rent
inflation is nearing its peak, consistent with the moderation
in asking rent growth. We expect rent inflation to ease to

3.6%yr by end-2026 and 2.5%yr by end-2027.
• New dwelling prices, which capture only the building
component of construction costs, are expected to rise
0.2%mth and 4.9%yr. Ongoing weakness in established
housing markets would be increasing builders’ difficulty in
passing through higher costs to new builds. As a result, we
expect new dwelling inflation to moderate to 4.5%yr by
end-2026 and 2.1%yr by end-2027.
Transport inflation is expected to rise 2.0%mth in September,

lifting the annual pace to 7.7%yr reflecting an 8.3%mth
increase in fuel prices following higher oil prices through the
month. Motor vehicle prices and spare parts are expected to
fall 0.7%mth and 1.3%mth, respectively. In NSW, a one-year
vehicle registration discount between $80-100 came into
effect from 1 September, which is likely to weigh on the other
services in respect of motor vehicles component.
Recreation & culture is expected to rise 2.1%mth driven by a

4.4%mth increase in holiday travel, partly offset by a 0.4%
decline in audio, visual & computing equipment & services and
a 1.3% fall in sporting services.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Risks to September baseline
We view the risks around the September CPI outcome as broadly 
balanced. Waning spending momentum outside of fuel may make 
it difficult for businesses to pass through higher costs in the near 
term. However, some of these pricing pressures may be deferred 
rather than forgone. The October ban on card surcharging may 
prompt a greater-than-usual degree of price resetting, allowing 
businesses to recover some of the margin pressures that have built 
up in recent months.
Risks to housing are mixed. Trade materials suppliers continued 
to announce above-average price increases through September 
(around 8%). While soft conditions in the established housing 
market may limit the extent to which these costs are passed 
through to new dwelling prices, they nevertheless present an 
upside risk to housing-related inflation, particularly maintenance 
& repair and insurance costs. Growth in advertised rents also 
continues to ease but these new leases take some time to flow 
through to average rents paid measured in the CPI. 
A kick up in October ...
We have a solid preliminary estimate for the October CPI of 
0.6%mth lifting the annual pace to 4.8%yr. It is true that the key 
driver is an 8.9% increase in auto fuel but we estimate the TM to 
lift 0.4%mth/3.7%yr highlighting broader inflationary pressures. 
We expect that the removal of credit charge surcharges and 
resulting increase in retail prices, at most, will contribute 0.1ppt 
but only just so see it as more of a rounding error. Nevertheless, 
these changes come at a time of a significant upstream price 
pressure, particularly for SMEs, so there is some risk of broader 
price adjustments. As such, we expect marginally larger than 
usual price adjustments in October particularly in hospitality 
and household services. Being the first month of the quarter the 
October lift sets a higher base for the quarterly average estimates 
resulting in an upgrade to our December quarter estimate.
We have included a table with a monthly profile for the CPI, 
seasonally adjusted CPI and the TM out to June 2027. At 
June 2027 we have an annual pace of 3.7%yr for the CPI and 
seasonally adjusted CPI and 3.3%yr for the TM. There is also a 
table breaking down the monthly components of the CPI through 
the December quarter. 
... highlights stronger inflationary pressure ...
Our September quarter CPI estimate is unrevised at 1.3%qtr 
with the annual pace steady at 3.9%yr. At the margin there 
was a 0.04ppt increase due to a lift in auto fuel but this is 
1.29%qtr at two decimal places so still rounds to 1.3%. 
The seasonally adjusted CPI gains 1.1% in the September 
quarter highlighting that part of the increase is due to usual 
seasonality. Our TM estimate for Q3 has a small downward 
revision of 0.03ppt to 0.88% at two decimal place so our 
published estimate is also unrevised at 0.9%qtr. 
... resulting in an upward revision to Q4 2026
Westpac’s December quarter CPI estimate has been boosted 
from a 0.5%qtr to 1.3%qtr. Driving most of this change from 
our previous estimate is the 15.3% increase in autofuel, it

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
was 1.9%, contributing 0.55ppt to the 0.8ppt increase in the 
quarterly estimate. Outside of a 0.09ppt contribution to the 
revision from holiday travel & accommodation (revised up from 
4.2% to 5.6%) all else was via small contributions for a large 
number of expenditure categories. This is why there is a 0.2ppt 
increase in our September quarter TM estimate from 0.7%qtr 
to 0.9%qtr.
A disinflationary impulse kicks in for 2027
We now expect auto fuel prices to peak in October (240 cpl 
for petrol, 284 cpl for diesel) and then to trend lower through 
2027 (184 cpl for petrol and 194 cpl for diesel by December 
2027) leading to a 13.6% falling in auto fuel in the first half 
of 2027 subtracting 0.46ppt off the CPI over the same period 
of time. For a detailed description on how we constructed the 
auto fuels profile please see: “ Persistent energy pressures 
have further to run ” 
The first half of 2027 will also see an almost 5% decline in 
holiday travel & accommodation which will subtract a further 
0.29ppt off the CPI. However, outside of that any broader 
moderation in inflation is expected to be more muted hence 
we have the TM printing 0.7%qtr for each quarter in 2027.  
Critical to this moderation in core inflation is our expect step 
down in dwelling price inflation on the back of falling estab -
lished home prices. From a 1.1%qtr increase in Q3 2026 we 
see dwelling price increasing by 0.7% in the Q4 2026, and 
then 0.6% in both Q1 and Q2 2027 before dropping to 0.5%qtr 
in Q3 and Q4 2027. This will see dwelling price inflation in the 
CPI drop from 5.4%yr at June 2026 to 3.0%yr by June 2027 
then down to 2.2%yr by December 2027. 
At least for the near term a key uncertainty remains the extent 
to which businesses are able to pass through ongoing labour 
and non-labour cost pressures and how this unwinds through 
2027 as energy prices ease and the domestic economy slows 
under the weight of higher interest rates and falling estab -
lished home prices. There is a risk that firms face greater 
resistance to price increases if discretionary household spend -
ing softens by more than expected. That said, these headwinds 
may be at least partially offset by the stronger starting point 
for household incomes, which has proven more resilient than 
previously anticipated, and by higher energy prices due to the 
ongoing conflict in the Middle East.
That said, there is also the risk that the disinflationary pulse by 
the second half of 2027 could be greater than what we antici-

pate. Another rate hike in November, along with a deeper than 
expected fall in established home prices, could be the prover -
bial “straw” resulting in a larger than expected fall in demand 
and thus a more significant moderation in price setting behav -
iour. It is also possible that our auto fuel prices fall by more 
than we anticipate. For while we have spot crude prices to fall 
to about pre-crisis levels by end 2027, a combination of a slow 
recovery in refining capacity, constrained return of physical 
supplies and higher shipping cost keep Australian fuel prices 
above their pre-conflict level. Should all these factors improve 
fast than we expect, and auto fuel prices return to pre-conflict 
level if not lower, then headline inflation could dip below 2%yr 
and the TM would be closer to 2.5%yr (the mid point if the 
RBA’s inflation target), by end 2027.

### Page 1

The Goldman Sachs Group, Inc.
Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh is steering the Fed into a less transparent era. So, is that
a positive or negative development, and what could it mean for markets and the
economy? We speak with Former Fed governors Donald Kohn and Stephen Miran
as well as GS’ Jan Hatzius who agree that rigid “Odyssean” forward guidance that
commits policymakers to a pre-set path risks policy mistakes and is therefore rarely
appropriate. But they somewhat disagree about the benefits of great er transparency

around the Fed’s reaction function. Hatzius and Kohn argue that such transparency
helps markets better anticipate and price in policy changes, accelerating monetary
policy transmission. But Miran believes that the line between committing to a path
and being transparent is clearer in theory than in practice. And while GS economists
and strategists find that reduc ed transparency leads to increased market volatility,
the question is whether this just increases the market’s noise or also improves the

market’s signal. Hatzius argues largely for the former and Miran for the latter.
Jan Hatzius, Chief Economist and Head of Global Investment Research, Goldman Sachs
“Markets price what they think the Fed will do, not what they think the Fed should do. That will remain true even if the Fed
obscures its reaction function… market guesses about the Fed’s next moves will simply become worse, leading to more
volatility... and importantly, volatility that serves no constructive economic purpose.”

Donald Kohn, former Vice Chairman of the Board of Governors of the Federal Reserve System, Robert V . Roosa
Chair in International Economics and Senior Fellow, Brookings Institution
“I sympathize with Chairman Warsh to some extent. [But] the real problem with Warsh’s approach is his reluctance to articulate
how the FOMC sees the economy evolving… explaining the Fed’s thinking about the economic outlook is essential in helping
markets act as a stabilizing force and is a core component of accountability.”
